([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/minimizer-based-mapping-block-length-computation))
- **minimizer-based-read-mapping**: Use when you have a draft assembly FASTA (e.g. celegans-abyss.fa) and long reads (e.g. SRR10028109 FASTQ.GZ) and need read-to-contig mappings faster than minimap2 alignments, in PAF format, for scaffolding or correction. - **minimizer-based-sequence-indexing**: Use when many short reads/transcripts must be approximately searched against very large collections of nucleotide sequences (e.g., hundreds of bins/genomes) where FM-indexes are infeasible and word-based indexes cannot handle edit-distance searches. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/minimizer-based-sequence-indexing))
- **minimizer-binning-directory-construction**: Use when a large nucleotide sequence collection (e.g., thousands of SRA human RNA-Seq experiments) must be indexed into bins so many queries (reads or transcripts) can be approximately searched for set membership faster and with less memory than full-text indices like Mantis or COBS. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/minimizer-binning-directory-construction))
- **minimizer-hash-table-genome-indexing**: Use when mapping long, noisy PacBio or Oxford Nanopore reads to a large reference genome and you need fast seed lookup plus detection of inversion SVs via anchor chaining. - **minimizer-index-construction**: Use when PacBio/ONT long reads (minimum length 1000 bp) must be mapped to a comprehensive reference database and approximate candidate mapping locations with estimated alignment identities are needed before probabilistic scoring. - **minimizer-index-construction**: Use when indexing very large nucleotide reference collections (e.g. NCBI RefSeq bins) for approximate read/sequence queries and memory, not CPU, is the binding constraint. - **minimum-experiment**: 最小实验提示语原文。用户纠结的事情靠继续想已经不会更清楚、需要用现实反馈来验证时使用。触发词包括 最小实验、先试试、怎么验证、别空想了、跑个实验、小成本试错、七天验证、已经反复纠结过很多轮、想了很久还是选不出来。产出待验证假设、一个低成本可逆实验的完整设计，以及明天就能做的第一个动作。 ([LearnPrompt/laoyeye](https://github.com/LearnPrompt/laoyeye/tree/HEAD/skills/deciding/minimum-experiment) · ★110)
- **minimum-experiment**: 最小实验（Minimum Experiment）：当一个想法、方案或关键假设停留在纸面推演、反复讨论也无法收敛时，用最低成本、可逆的一次真实试错替代空想，提前拿到现实反馈。先列出决定成败的假设，选出最关键、最不确定的那个，设计一个能在短周期内证伪它的最小实验，并预先定好观察指标、继续信号、停止信号和时间盒。理论基础是精益创业的「构建-测量-学习」循环、可证伪性（Popper）与探针式开发（Spike）。触发：「别空想了，先小规模试试」「帮我设计个能快速验证的实验」「这个想法怎么用最低成本试出来」「先做个原型/探针看看可不可行」「不确定要不要做，怎么先验证」「帮我挑出最关键的假设」「定好什么结果就继续/就停」「给我设个停损线再动手」。不适用：已有定论只需执行的任务、线上代码 bug 与崩溃验证（用工程环境自带的测试/TestSpec 流程）、给想法打分决定优先级（走 opportunity-scorecard）、两个方案抉择（走 steelman-choice）、写正式 PRD/计划（走 product-brief）、没有要验证的假设只是想干活。 ([Job-Yang/jobyang-ai-skills](https://github.com/Job-Yang/jobyang-ai-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/minimum-experiment) · ★103)
- **minimum-experiment**: 最小实验。识别最需要验证的假设，设计一个低成本、可逆的实验，用现实反馈代替空想。用于：纠结于选择、想验证假设、避免长期空转。 ([leebro7/Leebro-Skills](https://github.com/leebro7/Leebro-Skills/tree/HEAD/mind-model-skills/models/10-minimum-experiment))
